    INNER JOIN [Tenants] AS [t] ON [c].[TenantId] = [t].[Id]
    WHERE [c].[RoomId] = @roomId AND [c].[Status] = N'DangHieuLuc'
) AS [s]
LEFT JOIN [Occupants] AS [o] ON [s].[Id] = [o].[ContractId]
ORDER BY [s].[Id], [s].[Id0], [s].[Id1]

    SELECT TOP(1) [c].[Id], [c].[CreatedAt], [c].[Deposit], [c].[ElectricUnitPrice], [c].[EndDate], [c].[MonthlyRent], [c].[NumberOfOccupants], [c].[RoomId], [c].[StartDate], [c].[Status], [c].[TenantId], [c].[WaterUnitPrice], [r].[Id] AS [Id0], [r].[Area], [r].[BuildingId], [r].[CreatedAt] AS [CreatedAt0], [r].[Description], [r].[ImageUrl], [r].[Price], [r].[RoomNumber], [r].[ServiceFee], [r].[Status] AS [Status0], [t].[Id] AS [Id1], [t].[CreatedAt] AS [CreatedAt1], [t].[Email], [t].[EmergencyContact], [t].[FullName], [t].[IdCardNumber], [t].[Phone], [t].[UserId]
